金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 9 月 14 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 8 月 CPI 公布後，市場對聯準會政策路徑的關注進一步升溫：美伊衝突持續升溫帶動市
場對原油供給的擔憂，推升國際油價維持高檔震盪，能源價格上漲也使通膨再度成為市場關
注焦點。美國 8 月 CPI 年增率為 3.4%，與前月持平，但月增率由 0.1%升至 0.4%；核心 CPI
年增率則降至 2.4%。此次數據顯示通膨回落進程並未逆轉，但能源成本攀升仍可能增加未來
物價壓力。儘管聯準會理事表示，若通膨維持改善趨勢，9 月傾向按兵不動，但油價上漲是

否影響後續降通膨進程仍有待觀察，使利率政策路徑與市場預期持續出現分歧。
◼ AI 基建需求持續擴張，台灣 8 月出口再創歷史新高：台灣 8 月出口達 824 億美元、年增
41%，刷新歷史紀錄，其中電子零組件出口首度突破 300 億美元，年增 58%。AI 伺服器、
高階晶片、資料中心設備與雲端建設需求持續強勁，成為出口與科技產業成長的核心動能。
◼ 胡賽武裝威脅帶動西德州油價突破 100 美元大關：胡賽武裝 9 月 8 日攻擊沙國能源設施，9
月 10 日再奪取鄰近曼德海峽的摩卡港，推升能源風險溢價。由於沙國已將部分原油改經紅海
延布港出口，亞洲航線仍須通過曼德海峽。胡賽勢力逼近關鍵航道，即使尚未封鎖海峽，也可
能降低航商與保險業者承運意願，增添紅海供應中斷疑慮，帶動西德州油價突破 100 美元。
市場展望
◼ AI 題材擴散，科技股中期向上趨勢料不變：近期股市修正主要來自美債殖利率攀升壓縮高評
價科技股現值，半導體族群股價表現承壓；然而，AI 變現題材接棒，資金由晶片端轉向軟體應
用層，預料 AI 應用題材將持續發酵。建議降低高評價、高擁擠與高度依賴遠期獲利族群的集

中度，此外，投資人可採取定期定額或是趁股價拉回時分批佈局具獲利潛力的台日股市。
◼ 3～10 年期美國公債與投資級債為債市首選：美國財政部擴大長天期公債回購 ， 一度帶動長端
殖利率回落，但通膨與需求數據仍具韌性，且就業市場表現穩健，使年底前市場升息預期再度
升溫。市場對美國財政赤字、公債供給及長期通膨仍要求較高風險補償，考量當前 3～10 年
期美國公債與投資級債能鎖定目前較高息收 ， 且其受長端財政風險影響也相對較小 ， 故現階段
3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第 2 季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值上修至 2.2%， 惟低於第1 季
之 2.5%。成長主要由個人消費及固定投資帶動，其中消費貢獻 2.51 個
百分點；惟進口大增 12.6%，致淨出口拖累 1.10 個百分點。另實質私人
國內最終銷售成長 4.6%，顯示內需仍具韌性。 
⚫ 9 月美國非農就業僅增 2.9 萬人，遠低於前月及近一年平均，失業率升
至 4.2%， 薪資增幅續降 ， 顯示企業聘僱動能轉弱 。 惟勞動市場尚未明顯
惡化，後續仍須關注就業降溫對聯準會利率決策之影響。 
⚫ 8 月美國職位空缺數由 733.5 萬個降至 707.9 萬個，職缺率降至 4.3%，
顯示企業徵才需求略為降溫；惟聘僱人數增至 519.2 萬人。主動離職及
裁員規模同步下降，反映勞工轉職意願與企業人力調整趨於審慎，勞動
市場供需大致維持平衡。 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數為 54.5，連續第九個月維持擴張。
新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示需求仍具韌性；惟生產、出
口及進口動能略為放緩，且原物料成本持續攀升，加上關稅政策、地緣
政治及供應鏈不確定性仍存，製造業仍面臨部分壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國 PCE 物價指數月增 0.3%、年增 3.4%，核心 PCE 月增 0.2%、
年增 3.0%， 顯示通膨大致持穩但短期物價壓力略升 。 另一方面 ， 個人消
費支出月增 0.9%、實質消費成長 0.6%，帶動儲蓄率降至 4.1%，顯示家
庭需求仍具韌性；後續須關注消費熱度及通膨對聯準會政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期聯準會官員立場趨於分歧，理事 Michael Barr 認為能源價格及 AI
投資熱潮可能延緩通膨降溫 ， 未來仍可能進一步升息 ； 惟John Williams、
Philip Jefferson 及 Michelle Bowman 等官員則主張暫無急於升息的必要 ，
傾向觀察後續經濟數據。加以 9 月非農就業明顯放緩，市場對 10 月升
息預期大幅降溫，焦點轉向 12 月會議。 
⚫ 台積電傳評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作赴德州設廠，雙方仍處討論階
段。合作可能由台積電主導營運，或由 Terafab 持多數股權、台積電提
供技術 。 若成真 ， 將擴大台積電美國布局 ， 並支援Tesla、SpaceX 及 xAI
晶片需求。

險有限 ； 惟近期新增就業明顯低於預期 ， 企業聘僱意願與職缺需求放緩 ，

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.5 
一、 美國—美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
2026 年第二季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值由前次估計之 1.5%上修至 2.2%，
惟仍低於第一季之 2.5%， 顯示經濟持續擴張但成長動能略有放緩 。 此次上修主要
反映投資、消費及政府支出優於原先估計。就成長結構觀察，個人消費支出仍為
主要動能，貢獻 2.51 個百分點，其中服務及商品消費分別貢獻 1.57 及 0.94 個百
分點；私人投資貢獻 0.82 個百分點，以設備投資及智慧財產權投資表現強勁，惟
庫存調整拖累 0.53 個百分點。對外貿易方面，出口成長 5.0%，但進口大幅增加
12.6%， 致淨出口拖累GDP 成長 1.10 個百分點 。 另反映內需基本面的實質私人國
內最終銷售成長 4.6%， 高於第一季之1.8%， 顯示民間需求明顯增強 。 物價方面 ，
第 2 季 PCE 及核心 PCE 物價指數分別上升 5.0%及 3.3%， 顯示通膨壓力仍偏高 。
整體而言，美國經濟仍由消費與固定投資支撐，惟進口快速增加及通膨黏著性，
仍為後續經濟前景的重要觀察重點。 
2026 年 9 月美國非農就業人數增加 2.9 萬人 ， 明顯低於上月修正後的13.3 萬
人，也低於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.5 萬人的水準；失業率小幅升至 4.2%，
整體仍維持在 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%的區間，反映勞動市場整體變化有限，惟新
增就業力道已轉趨疲弱。家庭調查方面，勞動參與率升至 61.8%，就業人口比為
59.2%，均較上月小幅上升；長期失業人數約 194.4 萬人，占總失業人口 27.1%，
大致維持穩定。產業別方面，主要產業就業變化有限，其中醫療保健增加 1.7 萬
人、營建業增加 1.1 萬人、製造業增加 0.9 萬人，金融活動則減少 0.7 萬人，政府
部門減少 1.7 萬人。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升至 37.81 美元，月增 0.1%、
年增 3.0%，平均每週工時維持 34.4 小時。另 7 月與 8 月新增就業合計下修 6 萬
人，反映前二月就業動能較初估疲弱。整體而言，9 月美國新增就業明顯放緩、
失業率小幅上升且薪資增速降溫，企業聘僱動能較先前轉弱；惟失業率仍維持於
近期區間，勞動市場尚未出現明顯惡化跡象，後續仍須關注企業聘僱需求是否持
續降溫，以及對聯準會利率決策的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings) 由上月修正後之 733.5 萬個減少
25.6 萬個至 707.9 萬個 ， 職缺率由4.4%降至 4.3%， 顯示企業用人需求略有降溫 ；

惟聘僱人數由 514.6 萬人小幅增加至 519.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.2%升至 3.3%。總離
職人數由 512.8 萬人減少至 507.0 萬人，離職率維持於 3.2%。就離職結構觀察，
主動離職(Quits)人數由 308.9 萬人降至 306.6 萬人，主動離職率維持 1.9%；裁員
與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges) 人數則由 170.2 萬人降至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由
1.1%降至 1.0%。產業別方面，職位空缺增加主要來自零售業、金融保險業、資訊
業及休閒餐飲業 ； 聘僱人數則以政府部門及製造業增幅較為明顯 。 整體而言 ，8 月
美國職缺數較前月下滑，反映企業徵才需求略有降溫，但聘僱活動仍維持穩定，
且主動離職與裁員規模同步下降，顯示勞工轉職意願及企業人力調整趨於審慎，
勞動市場供需仍大致維持平衡。 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 為 54.5，
較上月之 54.6 微降 0.1 點，惟已連續第九個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，顯示製
造業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由53.7 升至 55.3，需求動能續增；生
產指數由 58.3 降至 56.7，雖仍維持擴張，但成長速度略有放緩；就業指數由51.2
升至 52.7，反映企業聘僱需求持續增加。供應商交貨指數由 59.3 微降至 59.0，交
貨延遲情況仍存；庫存指數則由 50.6 降至 48.6，企業備貨態度轉趨保守。此外，
價格指數由 71.1 升至 77.9，原物料及投入成本壓力升高；未完成訂單指數由51.8
升至 56.4，訂單去化變慢。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由53.2 降至 50.9，進口指
數亦由 52.5 降至 51.0，外需成長動能及企業備料需求略為放緩。整體而言，9 月
美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示製造
業景氣仍具韌性；惟出口及進口增速放緩、投入成本壓力升高，加上關稅政策、
地緣政治風險及供應鏈不確定性仍存，後續發展仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 8 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增率由上月之 0.1%升至 0.3%，
年增率維持於 3.4%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率由 0.1%升至 0.2%，年
增率亦持平於 3.0%，顯示整體通膨大致持穩，惟短期物價漲勢較前月略有回升。
另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)月增 0.3%，實質可支配所得持
平；個人消費支出月增 0.9%，實質個人消費支出成長 0.6%，顯示民間消費表現
明顯回溫。由於消費增幅高於所得增幅，個人儲蓄率由前月之 4.6%降至 4.1%。
整體而言，8 月美國通膨表現大致持穩，但消費支出顯著回升，反映家庭需求仍
具韌性；後續仍須持續關注消費熱度、通膨走勢及其對聯準會貨幣政策路徑之影
響。

近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標 ， 未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性 ； 惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、

月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示 「目前僅在討論階
段 ， 但可能會有進展」 。 目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠 ，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權 ， 台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗 。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨 、 核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%， 主因國際原油價格上漲 ， 使能源通膨上升4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含 ：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%， 失業人數增加2.6 萬人 ，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過 ，基於PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%， 較第一季降0.6 個百分點 ， 主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

環保法規，須負擔高昂翻修成本，使法國房價呈現連三季下滑所拖累。 
 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算 ， 也可能面臨倒閣風險 ；此外 ，市場對於2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。 惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長 ， 加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限 ， 反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐 。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險 ，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰國、菲律賓出口均達 2 成以上的高速成長

## Document 066: 2330台積電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-04
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

NYPCB

Alarm cleared with new drivers into 2027; 
re-it. Buy with PO raise to NT$2,000

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,000 TWD | Price: 1,340 TWD

BT substrate price hike resumed from 4Q26 
Following (see report) the worries of moderating BT substrate price hike in early 
September, we now expect NYPCB to still raise relevant ASP by single-digit % in 4Q26, 
and the hike is purely driven by favorable S/D which will fully translate into the accretion 
in bottom-line. Following this, BT substrate price will likely be raised by another 20+% in 
1Q27, due partly to the cost pass-through. We currently bake in ~10% revenue QoQ with 
32-33% GM for 4Q26, but we do not rule out the possibility for its revenue to surprise 
on the upside, should there be a faster ramp of its ABF substrate capacity in Kunshan. 
New driver for 2027: server CPU & mix optimization  
Looking into 2027 we expect the benefits of demand overflow on (see report) server 
CPU side to materialize, and we flag a chance for the firm to record 20+% revenue from 
the emerging business with AMD server CPU, where NYPCB is working on the 
qualification in its Shulin site currently and should capture most of the spillover demand 
if the progress goes smooth. Meanwhile, the firm will likely allocate more capacity from 
other sites (e.g. Kunshan) to fulfill the demand from Broadcom, which also helps enhance 
the mix. Currently we model ~50% GM for its ABF substrate business in 2027, but we 
see chance for further upward revision, driven by more favorable S/D dynamics and mix.  
Raise estimates/PO; prime beneficiary of price hike 
NYPCB’s share price (up ~50%) lags its major peers Unimicron/Kinsus (up ~70%) since 
late-July, but in our view it is still the best proxy to position for price hike in the 
substrate market. Judging from (see report) Kinsus’ August prints last week, we believe 
NYPCB remains on track to meet or beat the market’s expectations on N-T margins, and 
we reiterate Buy on the stock. Along with 13%/18% EPS raise in 2027/28E thanks to the 
more benign pricing/margin outlook driven by further mix improvement and S/D, we 
raise PO to NT$2,000 (from NT$1,700) same based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1997, NYPCB was listed on the Taiwan Stock 
Exchange in 2006. The firm is a 67%-owned subsidiary of 
Nan Ya Plastics Corporation (1303 TT). NYPCB's main 
products are HDI, PCB and IC substrates (including ABF and 
BT substrates), widely used in telecommunication, 
consumers, PC, and Auto applications. The clients include 
AMD, Broadcom, Samsung, Apple, Continental, etc. The 
main competitors include Unimicron, Kinsus, Ibiden, 
Shinko, and SEMCO, etc.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on NYPCB, in view of its continuous 
improvement of utilization rate and gross margin across 
ABF and BT substrate business into 2027, thanks to the 
robust demand growth from fabless customers and 
demand overflow in AI ASICs.

Price objective basis & risk 
NYPCB (NANYF) 
Our PO of NT$2,000 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E. We view this multiple as fair 
given ROE and operating margin expansion to 39% and 41% in 2027-28E (vs 13% and 
14% in 2025-26E).

Upside risks to our PO are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) higher SiP/AiP adoption, and (3) 
less intensified competition pressure in PCB business.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) muted SiP/AiP adoption, 
and (3) more severe competition in PCB business.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment rating s are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay  flat MoM in October: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75"
TV panel prices are expected to stay flat for the month of October. While panel
makers had previously notified their brands customers of potential price hikes from
4Q26 to mitigate elevated component costs, we believe that negotiations are
ongoing, as TV panel order momentum might slow down in the coming months on

seasonality and TV brands could reduce orders on higher panel prices. On the other
hand, panel makers continue to demonstrate good discipline, since Chinese panel
makers are committed to annual maintenance during the October holiday to control
output. We think it seems more likely that we will see TV panel price hikes toward
late 4Q26 when TV brands start to restock for CNY promotions. Our base-case
assumption is for the TV panel prices  to be flat QoQ in 4Q26, on a quarterly average

basis.
IT panel prices to remain flat MoM in October: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to be flat MoM during the month. Both applications continue to  see
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about
increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 4Q26.

Risk/reward  less attractive: We maintain the view that the current valuation of
Taiwanese panel makers, AUO (2409.TW) at 2.0x P/B and Innolux (3481.TW) at 1.7x
P/B, have already baked in the expectations of glass in advanced packaging for
revenue contribution in 2028. However, some supply-chain players think this might
take longer, and there seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying
for this opportunity. In China, while expectations are also building for advanced

packaging, we have a    relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL
(000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale  and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe current valuations of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in expectations
of glass in advanced packaging from 2H28 for the panel makers.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition remain key to watch.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trended Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance
following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.

Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to

profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Largely Fair
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

last 12 months. Due to rounding off of decimals, the percentages provided in the "% of total" column may not add up to exactly 100 percent.
Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

營收走勢圖

觀光事業  
八方雲集(2753.TT) 
台灣穩健成長，美國快餐新模式開啟新引擎

研究部預估八方雲集 2026 年 EPS 14.07 元(+12.2%YOY)，2027 年
15.05 元(+9.5%YOY)，對八方營運仍持正面看法，主要是考量：(1)下半
年肉品價格有所回落 ，成本壓力減輕， 加上公司嚴格控管美國地區費用，
下半年營業利益將能改善 ；(2)美國市場仍是公司中長期業績成長的主要
動能。儘管德州門市延後至今年 9 月才開幕，但快餐新模式初期單店營
收與營運效率均優於預期，有望改善美國事業營運業績。研究部給予 15
倍本益比，目標價 225 元，維持買進評等。

1. 雙品牌展店穩步推進，台灣營運維持成長 
八方雲集與梁社漢持續在台灣展店，截至2026 年 8 月底門市分別達
1,058 家及 260 家 ， 較去年底淨增25 家及 12 家 。 既有門市表現亦穩
健，1～8 月八方與梁社漢同店銷售分別成長約 5%及 6%。 未來公司將
持續開發科技園區、辦公大樓及封閉型商圈，並擴大 To Go 小型店
型，以提高市場滲透率。研究部預期台灣區 2026 年、2027 年營收將
分別成長 8.3%YoY 與 5.0%YoY。

2. 美國市場展店增加，營運有望改善 
美國事業目前仍處於快速擴張期 ，2025 年底總店數達 13 家，2026 年
上半年在加州新增 3 家門店，9 月底達拉斯開幕 ，之後德州還有 2 家
門店預計在年底前開出，預期至 2027 年將至少新增 5 家，屆時明年
底總家數有望達到 25 家。研究部預估美國區今明兩年營收都將有約
40%的增速，令2027 年美國事業虧損幅度收斂，並有望於 2028 年實
現年度損平。

3. 快餐新模式初步驗證成功，具跨區複製潛力 
達拉斯快餐新模式不僅提高出餐速度 ， 也可簡化人力與教育訓練 ， 初
期營運表現優於公司原先預期 。 公司計畫將此模式複製至美國新店 ，
甚至改造部分效率較低的加州門市，有望成為未來美國展店與改善
業績的重要模式。

一、營運概況：

八方雲集仍以台灣市場為公司主要營運重心 ， 美國市場則是逐步提高營收
占比。上半年品牌營收比重分別為：八方雲集 74.7%、梁社漢 24.8%、芳
珍 0.4%、丹堤 0.1%。

圖一、八方雲集 1H26 區域營收比重、品牌營收比重

資料來源：法說資料

台灣市場：雙品牌展店提速，數位點餐創造更多客流 
台灣市場為八方雲集最重要的營運與獲利支柱 。受惠於雙品牌規模經濟發
酵 與 中 央 工 廠 效 益 提 升， 台 灣 地 區 1H26 營收 上 升 至 35.60 億元
(+7.1%YoY)，獲利5.98 億元(+22.3%YoY)，獲利增速顯著優於營收。這波
成長動能延續到下半年。截至 2026 年 8 月底，主品牌「八方雲集」台灣
門市達 1,058 家，較去年底淨增 25 家； 「梁社漢排骨」達260 家，較去年
底淨增 12 家，其中加盟店占比已提升至 84%。

台灣市場仍是 八方雲集 主要營運
重心，上半年台灣區營收比重達
77.5%

八方雲集在台灣市場的餐飲品牌
以加盟店較多。海外市場則是以
直營店為主。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 3

此外，數位化轉型效益逐步顯現，線上點餐系統於加速導入後，有效縮短
尖峰時間的候餐期 ， 並提升門市整體服務量能。在今年尚未調漲售價的前
提下 ，1～8 月八方雲集與梁社漢的同店銷售增長率分別達 5%與 6%， 顯示
成長動能主要來自來客數與客單價的雙重提升，整體營運維持穩健。

圖三、八方雲集台灣區(年底)門店數量變化

美國市場：業務進軍德州，營收增速保持強勁 
美國市場自 2022 年 3 月以南加州為起點，設立加州中央工廠，並以直營
方式陸續展店， 已成為八方雲集目前最重要的海外擴張重心 ， 隨著新店持
續開出，營運規模正快速放大。在總門店家數由去年底的 13 家擴增至 16
家的帶動下，美國地區 1H26 營收則提升至 4.38 億元(+51.7%YoY)，展現
強勁的成長動能。

位於德州的達拉斯首店於 9 月正式開幕，將全美營運門市推升至 17 家。
目前德州另有 2 家門市正在裝修，預計年底前開出，2026 年底總店數至
少達到 19 家，若其餘送審門市進度順利，更有機會衝刺 20 家大關。

截至 2026 年 8 月底，主品牌「八
方雲集」台灣門市達 1,058 家，
較去年底淨增 25 家； 「梁社漢排

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

受固定成本與前期投入影響，1H26 美國營運尚處小額虧損階段 
美國子公司 1H26 虧損 5,700 萬元，主要是因為營收規模尚不足以完全吸
收固定成本，相關影響包括：(1)加州有額外成本支出。管理層表示，去
年北加州門市開出後，加州區域營運已接近損平，但 1H26 有 3 家新店延
後設計及送審，造成近 1 千萬元的前期支出；(2)為取得跨州供應資格，
加州中央工廠導入 USDA 規範，初期生產排程受限，且清潔流程、人力配
置要求提高，在生產效率下降時，成本亦同步增加。管理層表示，USDA 規
範導入初期的磨合階段已大致完成，目前生產效率正逐步改善 。(3)在達
拉斯首店於 9 月開幕前，持續投入的前期人力、租金等籌備支出。

德州廠產能到位，聚焦 USDA 認證與跨州物流戰略 
德州中央工廠已完工並正式投入生產，目前產能約可支援 30 至 50 家門
市 ， 因此短期內美國沒有重大資本支出的規劃。之後德州中央工廠亦將申
請 USDA 認證資格，屆時該廠食材將能採跨州冷凍配送供應至其他門店。
沿德州至加州物流路線積極洽談新店點(如在亞利桑那、 內華達等)，進一
步加快展店速度並產生規模經濟。

圖五、八方雲集美國區季度營收變化

資料來源：法說資料

香港市場：景氣偏弱策略審慎，精簡門市汰弱留強 
香港市場受當地消費景氣偏弱影響 ， 近兩年營運策略轉向審慎 ， 展店重心
轉為汰弱留強， 使香港地區1H26 營收下滑至 5.98 億元(-11.1%YoY)，1H26
獲利亦下滑至 1,570 萬元(-16.0%YoY)。雖然仍維持獲利，但受到門市營
收下滑及高租金壓力影響 ，利潤空間受到擠壓 。此次調整主要是關閉租金
成本過高的門市，故截至 2026 年 8 月底，集團在香港各品牌門市合計下
滑至 71 家，較 2025 年底淨減少 7 家。

美國地區1H26 營收則提升至4.38
億元(+51.7%YoY)， 展現強勁的成
長動能。

圖七、八方雲集台灣、香港、美國事業年度損益/近兩年上半年損益

資料來源：法說資料

二、業績回顧：

1H26 業績依舊有偏強增速，惟 2Q26 毛利率因成本壓力而下降 
八方雲集 2026 年上半年合併營收達 45.96 億元，成長 7.3%YoY；1H26 營
業利益達 5.56 億元，成長 15.4%YoY；1H26 稅後淨利 4.67 億元，成長
20.5%YoY；1H26EPS 7.01 元 。獲利增速高於營收表現，主要是受惠台灣業
務的規模經濟顯現 、 自動化中央工廠效益提升，令1H26 毛利率升至36.0%，
年增 0.4 個百分點；費用率則降至 23.9%，年減 0.5 個百分點。

單季度來看 ， 八方雲集2Q26 合併營收23.33 億元(+3.1%QoQ，+10.7% YoY)；
營業利益 2.72 億元(-4.0%QoQ，+13.0%YoY)，稅後淨利 2.30 億元(-
2.9%QoQ，+19.4%YoY)，2Q26EPS 3.45 元，略低於 1Q26EPS 3.56 元。

香港地區 1H26 營收下滑至 5.98
億元(-11.1%YoY)，1H26 獲利亦下
滑至 1,570 萬元(-16.0%YoY)。

台灣市場為公司主要 獲利來源 ，
1H26 獲利占比逾 90%， 成長22.3%

16.0%YoY； 美國1H26 呈小額虧損

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

獲利較前季下滑，主要是因為：(1)公司未調漲台灣區售價，但肉品價格
上漲，包材及運輸成本亦上升，壓縮利潤空間；(2)加州中央工廠因導入
USDA 規範而使生產效率下降。工廠需增加清潔程序 、 生產人力 ， 且生產排
程受到更多限制，亦增加部分成本，進而使2Q26 毛利率下降至 35.6%(季
減 0.8 個百分點，年減 0.4 個百分點)。

第 2 季推銷費用金額雖有增加，但因台灣區營收成長較快，2Q26 推銷費
用率下降至 17.9%(季減 0.1 個百分點，年減0.9 個百分點)。第2 季管理
費用較去年同期增加約 2 千萬元 ，主要來自德州前期籌備 、 人員進駐等支
出。管理層表示下半年仍會嚴控費用 ，預期費用不會較上半年出現明顯跳
升。

圖八、八方雲集 季度合併營收與毛利率表現

資料來源：法說資料

三、營運展望：

台灣市場：聚焦特定商圈展店，擴大 To Go 新店型深化滲透 
管理層認為台灣市場仍具進一步的展店與滲透空間 ， 未來將持續推進擴張
步伐。在選址策略上，公司將精準鎖定科技園區、辦公大樓、學區及封閉
型商圈進行開發。此外，公司將大力擴大布局「To Go 」小型外帶店。此
店型藉由縮小坪數 ， 能有效降低裝潢與人力成本 ， 不僅能大幅提升加盟主
的加盟意願 ， 更有利於切入過去因高昂租金而難以進駐的精華商圈 。 在門
市穩健增加的動能帶動下 ，研究部預期八方雲集台灣區今明兩年營收將分
別溫和增長 8.3%YoY 與 5.0%YoY。

2Q26 毛利率下降至 35.6%，主要
是因為國內肉品價格 上漲，加上
加州中央工廠額外成本支出。

美國市場：達拉斯快餐模式初步驗證成效，2027 年展店能見度高 
美國市場展店能見度已成功延伸至 2027 年。目前德州與亞利桑那州鳳凰
城已分別完成 3 家及 2 家 、共計5 家新店的簽約 。 考量美國新店從簽約到
開幕約需 7～8 個月 ， 此5 家門市預計將於 2027 年陸續加入營運 ； 同時 ，
公司也持續在加州北部與聖地牙哥等區域尋找新據點 。 在展店效益持續發
酵下，研究部預估今明兩年美國區營收將維持在 40%左右的強勁增速。

在營運效益上，八方雲集於達拉斯首店首度導入「快餐模式」 ，以批次製
作搭配流水線取餐，每小時可服務高達 100～120 位顧客。試營運期間僅
午餐 3 小時營收便達約 5,000 美元。管理層預估全天營業後 ， 日均營收可
望衝上 1.0 萬至 1.5 萬美元 ， 初期表現顯著優於加州門市的原先假設 。 若
此新模式持續驗證成功，公司計畫進一步導入加州部分門市 。

由於德州中央工廠已於 9 月正式投產 ， 短期美國事業因新店籌備 、USDA 規
範 、 物流及德州中央工廠固定成本先行投入等因素影響， 預期仍會呈現小
額虧損。然而，隨 2027 年展店家數增加、區域門市密度及中央工廠稼動
率提升 ， 固定成本可望逐步攤薄 ，尤其新快餐模式有望降低德州工廠所需
的損益兩平店數，預期2027 年美國事業虧損幅度將能收斂，並有望2028
年實現年度損平。

香港市場：大規模閉店告終，但策略仍謹慎，展望平淡 
香港市場上半年的結構性調整主要為關閉租金成本過高的門市 。 目前這一
波大規模的閉店調整已大致告一段落 ，未來集團不會再積極縮減店數 ， 但
仍會持續檢視單店效益 ，追求單店獲利能力的改善。考量香港市場未來策
略仍偏謹慎保守，故研究部預期香港區營收今年營收衰退 10.0%YoY，明
年衰退 3.0%YoY。

四、獲利預估：

下半年營收逐季升，且成本壓力趨緩，預期本業獲利將改善 
展望 2026 年，台灣「八方雲集」與「梁社漢排骨」展店動能優於預期，
預期台灣區營收仍維持穩健成長。美國方面，9 月達拉斯首店開幕後，還
會再新增 2~3 家門市，將能帶動合併營收逐季提升。雖 2Q26 毛利率受到
肉品、包材成本上漲影響，但隨下半年原物料價格趨穩，加上公司嚴控費
用，盈利能力有望逐步改善。研究部預估 2026 年合併營收 95.20 億元
（+8.3%YoY ） ，毛利率35.9% 、費用率 23.6% ，營業利益 11.73 億元
（+8.8%YoY） ，稅後淨利9.61 億元（+12.2%YoY），EPS 14.07 元。

美國進入加速展店期，新模式效果逐步顯現 
展望 2027 年，台灣市場聚焦八方與梁社漢兩大主力品牌，將繼續拓點，
滲透更多商圈 ； 美國市場得益於門市數持續增加 ， 以及快餐模式可望成為
後續美國展店的新標準，擴大營收規模。研究部預估 2027 年合併營收
102.33 億元 （+7.5%YoY） ，毛利率回升至36.0%，費用率降至23.1%，營業
利益 13.23 億元（+12.8%YoY） ，稅後淨利10.52 億元（+9.5%YoY），EPS 
15.05 元。

五、投資建議：

考量八方雲集台灣本業下半年業績將能改善 ，4Q26 營收與獲利還會創高 ，
加上美國市場營收成長且中期有望實現獲利，研究部給予 15 倍本益比。
目標價 225 元，維持買進評等。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

公 司 認 可 之 資 料來 源，並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷，但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性，
報 告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象
提 供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見，不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其
他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證
券 、 投 資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財
務 狀 況 與 投 資 條 件， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見。本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信
為 可 靠 之 資 料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正
確 性 作 出 任 何 陳 述 或 保 證。本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知。
本 投 資 報 告 並 非（ 且 不 應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經
紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或 公 司，為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說。 本 投 資 報
告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之 著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

## Document 209: Cliff Notes_ searching for signal (2026-10-15)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ searching for signal (2026-10-15).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: searching for signal
By Sian Fenner, Ryan Wells & Illiana Jain
13:33 October 02 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, this week’s main event was the RBA Monetary Policy Board’s (MPB) decision to raise the
cash rate by 25bps to 4.60%. This outcome was universally expected given Governor Bullock had
already communicated that upside risks to inflation had materialised – the conditionality established by

the MPB back in August for considering another rate hike.

### Page 2

Governor Bullock made explicit note of three upside risks. The Middle East conflict was emphasised as
the risk that had “materialised”, given the re-escalation and renewed surge in crude oil and refined
product prices over recent months. The RBA’s liaison warns this could lead to further pass-through of
costs and more persistent inflation pressures. On top of this, domestic capacity pressures and the data
centre/AI investment boom were also noted as upside risks that the MPB remain alert to.

In a video update mid-week, Chief Economist Luci Ellis explains why the bar for a follow-up rate hike in
November is low. We now expect a 25bp increase in November, provided there is no sudden resolution
of the Middle East conflict beforehand, or some other event that significantly lowers the outlook for
energy-related costs.
Subsequently, the August CPI revealed that underlying (trimmed mean) inflation rose 0.2% (3.4%yr),
which was slightly below the market’s expectation but spot on Westpac’s forecast. As usual, the

monthly detail was mixed, but the key drivers of underlying inflation remain centred on housing-related
components, particularly rents and new dwelling purchases. Headline inflation remained volatile, with
the unwind of the fuel excise cut causing another spike in fuel prices. We have maintained our forecast
for a 0.9% lift in trimmed mean inflation in Q3, and with upside risks building for Q4, a follow-up rate
hike in November appears most likely.

Regarding the current growth pulse, household spending surprised with a flat reading in August.
Services spend fell back following recent strength that was driven by major sporting events, but this
was offset by an increase in goods spend, particularly in fuel and electric vehicles. Together with our
latest card data for early September, the consumer spending pulse is shading a touch softer. However,
another solid outcome for business investment could once again be on the cards for Q3. August’s

goods trade data showcased another significant spike in data centre-related equipment imports, up
$4.3bn, similar in scale to Q1. While clearly import-driven, our modelling suggests that the local
economic impact of such investment is likely to be around half of the total investment.
Before moving offshore, a final note on housing. Cotality’s latest update revealed that dwelling prices
fell another 1.1% nationally in September. While Sydney and Melbourne have led the correction to

date, the fact that the other capitals are also reporting price declines clearly indicates that the
correction is deepening and broadening, as higher interest rates collide with the tax policy changes
announced in May’s budget. Sentiment effects indeed remain at play, as evinced by the continued pull-
back in investor credit growth. Westpac anticipates a peak-to-trough decline of around 7%, with tight
on-market supply limiting the extent of the correction. For more, see our latest Housing Pulse.

Offshore, there was plenty of data to dissect.
In the US, inflation eased in August despite ongoing strength in household demand and higher energy
prices. Headline PCE inflation rose 0.3%mth and 3.4%yr, while core PCE increased 0.2%mth and
3.0%yr. Encouragingly for the FOMC, the share of PCE components rising by more than 3% over the
past year, a measure frequently cited by Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh, declined to 45% from 49%,
suggesting some easing in broad-based inflation pressures. At the same time, household spending

remained robust, increasing 0.9%mth, equivalent to around 0.6%mth in real terms. That said, personal
income growth was soft, rising 0.2% in the month, signalling households opted to draw down savings.

### Page 3

The ISM manufacturing survey was stronger than the headline suggested. While the index edged down
0.1pt to 54.5, manufacturing has now expanded for nine consecutive months. There were some signs
of capacity constraints emerging, with production softened in the month but backlogs up 4.6pts to 56.4,
suggesting that firms struggled to keep pace with demand. New orders rose 1.6pts to 55.3, primarily
due to domestic demand as new export orders eased, backlogs increased. Firms responded by lifting

employment, 1.5pts to 52.7. Against this backdrop manufacturers saw a renewed acceleration in cost
pressures, with the prices paid index jumping 6.8pts to 77.9. Firms cited higher steel and aluminium
prices, elevated energy costs, rising freight charges and ongoing semiconductor shortages, with every
major industry reporting higher input costs during the month.
The past week’s data will have done nothing to bridge the clear divide across the FOMC, as evident in

this week’s busy Fedspeak. Some participants argued for further rate hikes, citing strong inflation,
diminished supply capacity and policy that is not yet restrictive. Others counselled patience, pointing to
progress on inflation and that September’s hike buys time to assess upcoming data. The one thing
both agree on is that inflation will be the primary determinant of the timing and extent of further rate
hikes. Our view remains that one further hike is likely this cycle to bring policy closer to neutral.

In China, policymakers announced another round of targeted support measures aimed at stabilising
growth and supporting the struggling property sector. The PBOC lowered rates on its Pledged
Supplementary Lending facility by 25bps to 1.5% and expanded quotas for targeted lending programs.
Authorities also introduced subsidies for eligible first-home buyers. The subsidy applies to homes with
a floor area of 120m² or less and a value below CNY1.5mn, with support capped at 1ppt of mortgage

costs, up to CNY10,000 and for a maximum term of five years. The measures complement reforms
announced in August to improve developer financing conditions and support housing completion.
However, weak credit demand and confidence remains a significant constraint, suggesting policy
transmission is still limited.
The September PMI data pointed to a modest improvement in activity, although the details were less
convincing. Manufacturing returned to expansionary territory, rising 0.9pts to 50.1, supported by

stronger production and new orders. Much of the improvement, however, reflected a rebound in prices,
with the raw materials price index rising 7.6pts and producer prices increasing 6.2pts. Labour market
conditions remained weak, with the employment index falling a further 0.6pts and remaining below 50
for almost four years. Conditions in the non-manufacturing sector also improved, with the services PMI
lifting from 49.0 to 50.2, driven largely by stronger construction activity. Input and output prices moved

higher, selling prices returned back above 50, export orders improved to 48.5 and employment
increased 0.5pts, although at 45.9 it remains firmly in contractionary territory. Overall, the surveys
suggest activity is stabilising and policy support is gaining some traction, but weak labour demand,
subdued credit growth and soft domestic demand indicate the recovery remains fragile.
In Japan, the September Tankan survey delivered another constructive signal on the economy and

reinforced the case for further policy normalisation. Business conditions improved by 2pts for large
manufacturers and 5pts more broadly, while profitability indicators strengthened and capital
expenditure intentions remained elevated. Large manufacturers expect fixed investment to increase by
11.6% in FY2026, while large non-manufacturers expect investment growth of 11.2%, pointing to

### Page 4

Related articles
continued confidence in the medium-term outlook.
Capacity indicators improved further across both manufacturing and non-manufacturing sectors, with
measures of excess production capacity becoming less negative and indicating that demand remains
sufficiently strong to keep pressure on existing capacity. Labour shortages also intensified, with
employment conditions becoming more negative across most industries and firm sizes, pointing to a

progressively tighter labour market. Inflation expectations remained anchored around 2%, providing
further evidence that Japan is moving towards a more sustainable inflation environment. Overall, the
survey was consistent with an economy operating close to capacity and supports the view that a
December rate hike remains a realistic possibility.

All that said, DB’s rates strategists now see French bonds as cheap relative to their
fundamental metrics. This chart looks at the residuals of France’s twin deficit score, which is a
weighted average of the historical current account balance (% of GDP) and budget balance (%
of GDP), with a higher weight assigned to more recent years.

EBO and CPO an ideal leverage of its capabilities 
Quantifying EBO and CPO opportunities; reiterate Buy
Action: Maintain Buy; raise TP to TWD1,415, implying ~34% upside
We expect AI optical interconnect and communication opportunities (e.g. co-packaged optics 
fiber array unit [CPO FAU], expanded beam optical [EBO], micro multi-fiber connector [MMC]) 
to transform the traditional optical lens industry over the next five years, and believe 
Taiwanese handset camera lens makers (e.g. Largan [3008 TT, Buy] and Genius) possess 
inherent advantages to cater to these new markets, given their high-precision molding+plastic 
injection, optical design, active alignment, optical coating and optical inspection capabilities, as 
well as clean-room facilities and sufficient free cash flow from cash-cow handset lens 
businesses.
In addition to holding the position of Apple’s (AAPL US, Not rated) No. 2 lens supplier, we believe 
Genius has obtained 3M’s (MMM US, Not rated) EBO ferrule order and will start shipping by end-
2026F or early-2027F. Genius appears well-positioned to supply various CPO FAU 
components (lens, prism, V groove, FA, etc.) and may potentially handle FAU assembly if 
requested by customers. We estimate the EBO and CPO-related new businesses will 
contribute <3% and 16-20% earnings for Genius in 2027F and 2028F, respectively (details 
in Fig. 10). Yet, given the high dollar content and long-term growth potential of CPO/FAU related 
products over the next five years, we believe optical lens makers with inherent advantages in this 
new field deserve stock re-rating; we thus raise our target P/E to 25x (near the high end of the 
historical range of 10-30x since 2020), from 17x. We reiterate Buy, and raise our TP to 
TWD1,415, based on 25x 2028F EPS of TWD56.61 (vs. prior TP of TWD568, based on 17x 
2025F EPS of TWD33.4, set two years ago). Genius trades at 25x 2027F P/E.
Apple: Sunny’s market share loss and continued camera upgrades in 2027-28F
Sunny (2382 HK, Neutral) earlier faced some yield issues with the new variable aperture (VA) 
main camera lens for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and this could benefit Largan (more directly) and 
Genius (indirectly) in the competitive landscape. It remains uncertain whether Genius will be 
introduced as a third lens supplier to iPhone Pro’ main camera in the future, and this poses an 
upside risk to our forecasts. However, given the headwinds of rising memory costs and the 
potential cannibalization of the 18 Pro series by the iPhone Duo, we forecast slower iPhone lens 
shipments from end-2026F to 1H27F, before the 20th anniversary iPhone (Pro Max) and the 
second-gen iPhone Duo are introduced in 2H27F. We expect further upgrades to the iPhone Pro 
main camera in 2H27F, and introduction of a new periscope camera for the Pro Max in 2H28F.

### Page 4

Optical communication – an ideal leverage of 
lens makers’ capabilities
Optical interconnect – from MPO to MMC and EBO
Multi-fiber push-on (MPO) is the current standardized physical-contact optical 
interconnect architecture designed to aggregate high-density ribbon fibers (typically 8, 12, 
16, 24 or 32 cores per ferrule) into a single compact interface. MPO connectors leverage 
precise physical contact enforced by a high mechanical spring force (around 10-20N) and 
steel guide pins for alignment to achieve ultra-low insertion loss (0.20-0.35dB) across 
clean fiber end-faces.
The micro multi-fiber connector (MMC) is another proprietary physical-contact optical 
interconnect standard beyond MPO developed by US Conec (unlisted) for high-density 
AI interconnects to directly address the server faceplate and panel space constraints. The 
MMC introduces a very small form-factor (VSFF) multi-fiber design that utilizes a 
miniaturized TMT ferrule (patented by US Conec; Fig. 1 ), and according to US Conec, 
the width of the TMT ferrule is similar to that of conventional MT ferrule yet with ~30% 
smaller in thickness and ~50% shorter in length to shrink the physical footprint to roughly 
one-third the size of an MPO. The spatial reduction consequently unlocks up to a 3x 
density advantage in patch panel ports and transceiver faceplates compared to standard 
MPO footprints (Fig. 2 ). Meanwhile, the MMC delivers a crucial density jump while 
preserving high optical performance, offering low insertion loss and supporting up to 
250um pitch multi-fiber ribbon arrays (including 12-, 16-, 24-, and 32-fiber configurations) 
to ensure interoperability with MPO ecosystems. The MMC also improves operational 
serviceability through US Conec’s DirectConec push-pull latching mechanism to allow 
technicians to plus and unplug individual cables even at a highly populated panel without 
external retraction tools.
Fig. 1:  TMT ferrule vs. conventional MT ferrule

Source: US Conec, Nomura research
Fig. 2:  MMC has a smaller footprint than MPO to enable a 3X 
optical interconnect density

Source: US Conec, Nomura research
Although MPO and MMC are the most popular standards of optical fiber connectors 
nowadays, they both rely on direct physical contact between ferrules and thus require 
higher spring pressure and precision alignments when connecting the cables. Also, 
physical contacts leave MPO and MMC’s exposed optical interfaces inherently 
susceptible to wear and tear over repeating mating cycles as well as potential signal 
degradation from dust and debris contamination (and therefore needs frequent cleaning 
and inspection).
Expanded beam optical (EBO), a patented technology by 3M (MMM US, Not rated; 3M 
owns over 100 patents on it, with 50+ patents pending), reshapes optical interconnect 
technologies by addressing pre-mentioned challenges of MPO or MMC, since EBO 
replaces traditional physical-contact end-face matings with non-contact, lens-based 
beam expansion (Fig. 4 ). EBO utilizes integrated total internal reflection (TIR) mirrors 
within precision polymer ferrules. As the light exits an optical fiber core and enters the 
ferrule, it strikes the internal TIR mirror that collimates and expands light beams up to 10x 
their core diameter before travelling through an intentional, tiny air gap to the mating 
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
4

### Page 5

connector. An identical, paired TIR mirror is integrated within the receiving ferrule to 
capture the expanded beam and focuses it back into the receiving fiber core. According to 
3M, the EBO ferrule is vapor-coated with a special anti-reflection coating that efficiently 
reduces the reflection of light beams (Fig. 5 ).
Fig. 3:  An EBO ferrule

Source: 3M, Nomura research
Fig. 4:  Connection of two EBO ferrules

Source: Rosenberger, Nomura research

Fig. 5:  A cross-section illustration of EBO

Source: 3M, EBO MSA, Nomura research

Although one of the underlying issues with EBO is a higher insertion loss (0.35-0.75dB), 
3M’s EBO connector system still provides a few advantages compared to traditional 
physical-contact connectors:
Physical-contact connectors require frequent inspection and cleaning cycle before 
mating, while EBO is nearly immune to airborne particle contamination thanks to the 
embedded lens system and no direct surface contacts. An easier handling with 
reduced maintenance efforts therefore significantly lowers the installation time (as 
much as by 85%, according to 3M) and labor cost.
•
EBO can ensure consistent signal integrity across thousands of mating cycles due 
to contact-less designs to avoid face-wear and micro-scratching. By contrast, physical 
connectors grind against each other and ultimately lacks long-term durability.
•
Traditional connectors require precise fiber-to-fiber alignments, whereas the EBO 
connector system can be used in a hermaphroditic condition (socket/connector 
agnostic) given identical connector at both ends. As such, the risk of installation 
error is largely eliminated, even without the need for fiber-optic specialists.
•
EBO only demand a fraction (e.g. one-tenth) of the high spring pressure that 
compresses glass end-faces together in physical connectors, thereby minimizing 
•
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
5

### Page 6

mechanical strains on chassis frames (demo video by 3M ). 
The contact-less, high density, blind mating, and low spring pressure features 
make EBO potentially an ideal optical connector for optical back-plane interconnect 
applications (similar to the function of orthogonal backplane or mid-plane in nVidia’s 
[NVDA US, Not rated] Kyber architecture), in our view.  
•
Fig. 6:  3M's current EBO ferrule offerings (12F/16F)

Source: 3M, Nomura research

The EBO Multi-Source Agreement (EBO MSA) was established in March 2026 to turn 
3M’s proprietary EBO technology into an open, industry-wide standard for AI cluster 
infrastructure. The primary objective is to standardize EBO connector mechanical form 
factors, latching mechanisms, and optical fiber interface dimensions to allow hyperscalers 
to source interchangeable EBO connectors from multiple vendors rather than relying on 
one single supplier. According to EBO MSA, there are 9 end-user companies (including 
Microsoft [MSFT US, Not rated], Meta [META US, Not rated], nVidia, AMD [AMD US, Not 
rated], etc.) and 48 vendors in the membership by the end of September 2026 (Fig. 7 ). 
Members vote on specific connectors to develop and contribute design specifications – 
there are 18 types under discussion, and four are on the roadmap:
128f (8x16f) design specification is underway.•
16f MPO design is under discussion on specification.•
16f VSFF and external laser small form factor pluggable (ELSFP) are planned, and 
we note that EBO development for ELSFP could be challenging since high-power 
laser could damage the AR coating and polymer ferrules.
•
Fig. 7:  EBO MSA member companies

Who are the potential adopters of EBO connectors?
Of course, all the key members of EBO MSA are potential users of EBO connectors. But 
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
6

### Page 7

for more concrete industry developments, 3M and Microsoft announced strategic
partnership in July 2026 (link ), and Microsoft was the first announced hyperscale cloud
provider to deploy 3M EBO technology in its Azure cloud. Microsoft’s early use of EBO
technology has shown the potential to reduce network deployment timelines in certain
environments. The technology has also demonstrated strong signal performance in live
data center conditions, where dust exposure and routine handling are typical during

installation and maintenance. The technology will help Microsoft reduce the need for
frequent cleaning and inspection while supporting reliable optical performance in dense,
high-volume deployment environments.
Meta joined EBO MSA in May 2026, and from our industry checks, we think Meta is likely
evaluating EBO technology for future ASIC mass-volume optical interconnect demand.
We also observe that AWS (AMZN US, Not rated), although it is not yet an MSA member,

could potentially evaluate this technology for its future ASIC mass-volume optical
interconnect demand.
Why optical plastic lens companies have advantages in
producing ferrules for passive optical connectors?
From MPO to MMC, the fiber density increases to 3x, and we notice that the precision
level requirements for MMC TMT ferrules also increase substantially versus MPO MT
ferrules. The high precision levels (<5um, or even <3um) of MMC TMT ferrules

usually exceed the traditional optical connector makers’ plastic injection capability
(usually >10um) and traditional connector makers need to invest new, higher precision
level tools to deal with those higher precision requirements. However, those precision
levels are about the right levels (or even lower-end than) which plastic camera lens
makers can do without much new investment in machine tools. This is why we think
plastic lens makers have advantage in the advanced passive optical connectors. The

reason why we do not see many lens makers enter MMC ferrule market at this moment is
because it is a patented technology by US Conec, and US Conec only licenses to limited
suppliers, such as Sumitomo (5802 JP, Buy), etc.
For EBO as well, although EBO ferrule looks complex in design and shape, it is a suitable
product for plastic camera lens makers to do. We estimate the difficulty and price of
EBO ferrule is around the levels of mid-range handset camera lenses.

3M likely outsources EBO ferrule to Genius
From our industry survey, 3M will likely outsource the EBO ferrule components to Genius,
which is done by high-precision plastic injection. Genius can leverage its handset camera
lens equipment and make new molds to produce the product. Hence, we do not expect
too much extra capex is required.
In our understanding, fiber connector makers can produce EBO after getting authorized
by 3M. We suspect that 3M will supply the key EBO ferrule to the authorized

manufacturers at least in the early stage of technology development. This may explain
why 3M comes to reach handset lens makers for the ferrule production.
Our base assumption for Genius’ EBO ferrule revenue contribution is about 2-3%
for 2027F and 2028F, by assuming an ASP of USD0.5-1 with 20-35mn units annual
volume. We assume the GM for this new product to be higher than corporate average.
The upside of EBO market will depend on the adoption and penetration of the technology

into hyperscalers’ AI optical interconnect roadmap. By our estimate, every rack
(containing 60-80 units of GPUs or AI ASICs), if adopting EBO for optical scale-up for
intra-rack or cross-rack interconnect technology, will potentially require 600-1,000
units of EBO ferrule components (300-500 pairs). Our base volume assumption for
Genius’ EBO shipments require one major CSP to adopt EBO in one major AI
platform, plus global sampling, yield loss, trial run demand and necessary

inventory buffer. If there are more than one major AI projects adopt EBO in 2028F, it will
represent potential upside to our forecasts.
CPO opportunities could be bigger than EBO, but less clear at
this moment
Unlike that EBO ferrule is a confirmed new business for Genius (though the market TAM
upside is unclear at this moment), CPO opportunities to Genius remain uncertain in
details, such as what products to offer, what are the business models, and who are

intermediate customers and/or end customers. Not to mention, the ramp-up of CPO
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
7

### Page 8

market itself also has high uncertainties.
We believe Genius is working with FOCI (3363 TT, Not rated) in supplying key 
components for CPO FAU, such as V groove, prism, and/or fiber array (FA). It is also 
likely in talk with other FAU makers for various component supply (including lens, V 
groove, prism, FA, etc.). These FAU makers tend to do FAU final assembly in-house, and 
hence, may treat Genius as a component supplier to fill its internal capability or 
outsourcing partners’ gap. Genius’ flexibility in various component offering for CPO FAU 
could make it an easier partner for FAU customers, versus Largan (it tends to do more FA 
or FAU, rather than just components), and may see revenue contribution earlier than 
Largan. However, we assume USD25-35 ASP for Genius’ CPO FAU component offering 
in our base assumptions, given that it may supply more components rather than doing 
finished FAU products (ASP USD>100).
In the other side, we also see opportunities for Genius to get FAU module business from 
customers like Corning (GLW US, Not rated), but the progress of Genius to do such full 
assembly lines could be slower than Largan, as Genius still relies more on external 
equipment supply, unlike Largan having strong internal equipment design and 
manufacturing capability.
We assume CPO FAU to contribute Genius’ EPS by <1% in 2027F and 17% in 2028F.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, subject to 
limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an appropriate 
valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow analysis, expected 
return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated target price, defined as 
Nomura | Genius Electronic Optical 5 October 2026
12

### Page 13

(target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that the 
analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 'N/A' are 
not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging Markets (ex-Asia): 
MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and by 
other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026

Exports and inbound demand remain solid despite weakness in some areas
◆ Goods exports are on the verge of picking up
Goods exports are treading water. A sharp rise in memory chip prices is among
factors pushing up export prices, resulting in record high levels of exports in value
terms recently. On the other hand, the increase in volume terms is limited. One

possible reason is that Japan's export competitiveness has declined in fields such as
semiconductors, where global demand is growing.
Looking ahead, exports are expected to pick up. Capital investment by chipmakers
is expanding worldwide, and this should lift exports of semiconductor manufacturing
equipment, an area in which Japan has strength. However, continued caution is
needed regarding the effects of prolonged turmoil in the Middle East. A global

slowdown due to elevated crude oil prices could weigh on exports.
◆ Reluctance to visit among Chinese travelers is weighing on demand for
travel to Japan
Inbound demand is currently soft. The Chinese government's advisory urging its
citizens to refrain from traveling to Japan is having an adverse impact. The number
of Chinese visitors to Japan has fallen to about half the level prior to the advisory.
Meanwhile, the number of tourists from countries other than China remains firm.

Inbound demand is expected to stay robust going forward. Continued increases in
tourists from countries other than China will remain a supporting factor. The
expectation that the yen will remain weak will also act to push up inbound
consumption. However, there are two downside risks going forward:
The first is that the Chinese government keeps the advisory in place for a
prolonged period. When China banned group travel to South Korea in 2017, tourist

numbers stayed low for two to three years.
The second is the impact of higher crude oil prices. Major airlines have embarked
on phased increases in fuel surcharges, which could weigh on inbound demand
during the summer.

### Page 6

Personal consumption is gradually recovering thanks to a favorable income environment
◆ Higher disposable incomes are supporting personal consumption
Personal consumption continues to gradually recover. While consumption of
non-durable goods remains weak, services consumption is firm, particularly in
amusement-related services. In addition, consumption of durable goods has
increased significantly. Temporary factors such as the abolition of the environmental

performance tax on automobiles (since April 2026) and the tightening of
energy-efficiency standards for air conditioners (effective April 2027) have also
contributed to this rise.
A favorable income environment can also be pointed to as a background factor
behind the recovery in personal consumption. YoY real disposable income growth has
generally been positive since mid-2024, and the pace of increase has recently
accelerated. Employee remuneration is rising, partly as a result of agreements made

during the shunto (spring wage negotiations), and net asset income is also increasing
at a faster clip as companies hike dividends in line with stock price rises.
◆ Stock price developments could be a source of concern for personal
consumption
Personal consumption is expected to continue its gradual recovery going forward. In
addition to the favorable income environment and government policy measures to
curb price increases, a recovery in consumer confidence driven by easing tensions in

the Middle East will also provide a tailwind. Recently, the Consumer Confidence Index
has been showing signs of bottoming out.
However, a reverse wealth effect stemming from falling stock prices is a potential
cause for concern. The Nikkei Stock Average, which reached a new high in June, has
been struggling to push higher since the start of July. Estimates suggest the wealth
effect is concentrated among elderly households, but in recent years working-age

households have also increased their holdings of equity-related assets, and the
positive wealth effect for them may have supported consumption to some extent. For
this reason, if stock prices were to fall significantly, there is a danger of downward
pressure on consumption across a wide range of households.

セン
To
◆ The unemployment rate has settled in the mid-2% range
While the employment situation is generally favorable, weakness can be seen in
some areas. The unemployment rate has remained in the mid-2% range since 2022.
The number of employed persons in non-manufacturing, where business conditions
are favorable, is holding firm, while in manufacturing the number remains at a level
below that seen before the COVID-19 pandemic.
In the background to the drop in manufacturing employment is a slump in

production activity. Manufacturing output has been shrinking due to the effects of
U.S. tariff policy, soaring raw material prices, and a decline in the international
competitiveness of Japanese firms. As a result, pressure to reduce headcounts has
been building. Even in non-manufacturing, some industries closely linked to
manufacturing, such as transportation and wholesale/retail, are also exhibiting
weakness in labor demand.
◆ The employment situation could remain fragile in the near term

Looking ahead at the employment situation in the short term, a sense of
stagnation may persist. The contraction in output in the manufacturing sector will
continue to weigh on labor demand. Although production activity has been brisker
recently, particularly in semiconductor-related industries, the employment-creation
capacity of such industries is small. The employment-inducement coefficient per unit
of new demand tends to be lower than in other sectors, meaning that the industries

lack the strength to serve as drivers of employment.
However, in the medium to long term, the unemployment rate is expected to see a
gradual decline. Demand for labor is projected to stay solid, especially in
face-to-face services such as medical/nursing care and accommodation and
eating/drinking services, but the number of people seeking employment from the
non-labor force population has declined significantly due to the expansion in elderly
and female workforce participation. With limited room for expansion in the labor

supply, the job market is expected to tighten further.

The government is curbing inflation
◆ Policy factors are pushing down the inflation rate
In June, core inflation (all items, less fresh food) was 1.6%, staying under 2%
for the fifth consecutive month. Food price inflation has slowed, and a major

reason for this is a fallback in rice prices, which soared last year. Gasoline
subsidies are also continuing to curtail energy price increases.
Inflation looks set to hover in the 2% range in the second half of 2026. Although
energy subsidies and other government measures will soften inflationary pressure
to some extent, with imported inflationary pressure building, prices are expected
to be pushed up as higher costs are passed through to selling prices. From

FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items will
serve to keep prices down, and inflation is expected to drop into the 1% range.
◆ The Bank of Japan (BOJ) will keep its policy rate unchanged
At its July monetary policy meeting, the BOJ decided to keep its policy rate
unchanged.
Long-term interest rates remain high. This reflects concerns about inflation
resulting from the increase in oil prices and expectations of an expansion in fiscal

expenditure.
The BOJ is expected to step up the pace of interest rate hikes while closely
monitoring the impact of higher rates on the real economy and financial/capital
markets. The next rate hike is expected to be in September.
Long-term interest rates look likely to rise gradually, reflecting heightened
anticipation of rate hikes by the BOJ and increased fiscal spending by the
government. If jitters about a deterioration in fiscal discipline resulting from such

measures as energy subsidies or the consumption tax cut take hold, there is also
a risk that long-term interest rates will rise.

◆ Surging crude oil prices have become a source of upward pressure on
prices
With crude oil prices having spiked against the backdrop of the worsening situation
in the Middle East, corporate input costs have risen sharply, and pressure for price
pass-through is intensifying. Looking at the Final Demand-Intermediate Demand
price indexes, the margin of price increases at the upstream stage far exceeds that at
the downstream stage, and price pass-through pressure is also substantial at the

downstream stage.
According to estimates based on past data, the impact of rising oil prices involves
a time lag. Specifically, the push-up effect on the consumer price inflation rate
increases for around 10 months and continues to exert influence for a long period
thereafter.
◆ Price pass-through willingness is also stronger than before
Notably, food product manufacturers are planning to raise prices for a huge
number of items during the summer and beyond. According to Teikoku Databank, the

number of items slated for price increases as of the end of July, when it conducted its
latest survey, was higher than in the May and June surveys.
If government subsidies for electricity/gas bills and gasoline prices are scaled back
or ended, both energy and food prices will increase concurrently, and the consumer
price inflation rate may rise again.
Attention also needs to be paid to the possibility that price pass-through driven by
elevated oil prices will become prolonged. In the 2020s, the rate of price increases in

transactions between companies has been higher than in the 2010s, and firms'
medium-term outlooks for selling prices have also shifted upward. Events that trigger
cost increases, such as heightened geopolitical tensions, have been occurring
repeatedly, and companies may be more willing to pass through higher costs to
prices than they were before.

### Page 10

The economy is expected to continue to recover, but downside risks are present
◆ The economy is projected to stay on the recovery track
The Japanese economy should continue its gradual recovery. WTI crude oil
futures prices are expected to fall moderately going forward.
In the corporate sector, government support for investment is providing a tailwind,
and demand for investment for labor saving and digital transformation remains
robust. In addition, rising global demand for AI-related goods should support exports.

Supply chain disruptions resulting from the deterioration in the Middle East situation
will begin to ease, and corporate production activity is expected to gradually recover.
In the household sector, inflationary pressure is expected to strengthen from the
summer onward due to higher crude oil prices. In response, the government will
probably ease the inflationary pressure through measures such as energy subsidies.
While the pace of increase in personal consumption is likely to slow somewhat,

consumption is expected to remain resilient amid continued high wage growth. From
FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items should also
support personal consumption.
◆ The Middle East situation and rising interest rates constitute risks
Real GDP growth is projected to remain solid, registering +0.8% in FY2026 and
+0.9% in FY2027.
However, if the situation in the Middle East worsens again or becomes prolonged,
there is a risk that downward pressure on the economy will intensify, mainly through

three channels: 1) weaker exports, 2) raw material supply constraints, and 3) rising
prices. In addition, if market concerns about the fiscal stance of the Takaichi
government grow, the yen could weaken further, and long-term interest rates could
rise, potentially forcing the scaling-back or scrapping of expansionary policies such
as investment support measures, energy subsidies, and lower consumption tax.
